`src/adapters/utxo/codec.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The bitcoinjs-lib codec (spec §15): the unsigned transaction and its PSBT (BIP174), the
 * per-input signature hashes (legacy, BIP143 for segwit v0, BIP341 key path for taproot),
 * assembly of the signed transaction, and the signatures of a PSBT signed elsewhere. The
 * driver persists only the PSBT (base64) and plain data (R11), and re-parses it here.
 */
import { bytesToNumberBE, numberToBytesBE } from '@noble/curves/abstract/utils';
import { secp256k1 } from '@noble/curves/secp256k1';
import { SigningError, ValidationError } from '../../core/errors/error';
import type { SignatureBundle, SigningRequest } from '../../core/signing/types';
import { concatBytes, equalBytes, fromHex, toHex } from '../../core/util/bytes';
import { hash160, outputScript, type WalletAddress } from './address';
import type { PlannedOutput, Spendable } from './coinselect';
import { bitcoin, useNobleEcc, type Network, type Psbt } from './sdk';

export const SIGHASH_ALL = 0x01;
export const SIGHASH_DEFAULT = 0x00;
/** BIP125 opt-in (and relative lock time disabled); `0xfffffffe` when RBF is off. */
export const SEQUENCE_RBF = 0xfffffffd;
export const SEQUENCE_FINAL_LOCKTIME = 0xfffffffe;

export interface PlannedInput extends Spendable {
  /**
   * The full previous transaction (BIP174 `non_witness_utxo`), txid-checked: required for
   * p2pkh, and added for segwit v0 so hardware wallets can check the input amounts (D12).
   */
  readonly prevTxHex?: string;
}

export interface BuiltTx {
  /** The PSBT, base64 (spec §15: the signing payload a cold signer receives). */
  readonly psbt: string;
  /** One signature hash per input, in input order. */
  readonly digests: readonly Uint8Array[];
  /** The txid, when every input is witness-type (it is then fixed before signing). */
  readonly txid?: string;
}

/** Internal byte order of a txid (bitcoinjs `addInput` takes the reversed display hex). */
const txidBytes = (txid: string): Uint8Array => fromHex(txid).reverse();

/** Checks a previous transaction's bytes against the outpoint, value and script it funds. */
function assertPrevious(prevTxHex: string, input: Spendable, script: Uint8Array): void {
  let prev;
  try {
    prev = bitcoin.Transaction.fromHex(prevTxHex);
  } catch {
    throw new ValidationError('INVALID_INTENT', 'a previous transaction does not decode');
  }
  const output = prev.outs[input.vout];
  if (
    prev.getId() !== input.txid ||
    !output ||
    output.value !== input.value ||
    !equalBytes(output.script, script)
  ) {
    throw new ValidationError(
      'INVALID_INTENT',
      'a previous transaction does not match its outpoint',
    );
  }
}

/**
 * The unsigned transaction (version 2, lock time 0) and its PSBT, spending `inputs` of the
 * wallet to `outputs` in the given order.
 */
export function buildTx(
  network: Network,
  wallet: WalletAddress,
  inputs: readonly PlannedInput[],
  outputs: readonly PlannedOutput[],
  sequence: number,
): BuiltTx {
  useNobleEcc();
  const tx = new bitcoin.Transaction();
  tx.version = 2;
  tx.locktime = 0;
  for (const input of inputs) tx.addInput(txidBytes(input.txid), input.vout, sequence);
  for (const output of outputs) tx.addOutput(output.script, output.value);

  const psbt = new bitcoin.Psbt({ network });
  psbt.setVersion(2);
  psbt.setLocktime(0);
  for (const input of inputs) {
    if (wallet.type === 'p2pkh' && input.prevTxHex === undefined) {
      throw new ValidationError(
        'INVALID_INTENT',
        'a p2pkh input needs its verified previous transaction',
      );
    }
    if (input.prevTxHex !== undefined)
      assertPrevious(input.prevTxHex, input, wallet.script);
    psbt.addInput({
      hash: input.txid,
      index: input.vout,
      sequence,
      ...(wallet.type === 'p2pkh'
        ? {}
        : { witnessUtxo: { script: wallet.script, value: input.value } }),
      ...(input.prevTxHex !== undefined && wallet.type !== 'p2tr'
        ? { nonWitnessUtxo: fromHex(input.prevTxHex) }
        : {}),
      ...(wallet.redeemScript ? { redeemScript: wallet.redeemScript } : {}),
      ...(wallet.type === 'p2tr' ? { tapInternalKey: wallet.publicKey } : {}),
    });
  }
  for (const output of outputs)
    psbt.addOutput({ script: output.script, value: output.value });
  if (!equalBytes(unsignedTxOf(psbt), tx.toBuffer())) {
    throw new SigningError(
      'SIGNING_FAILED',
      'the PSBT does not encode the built transaction',
    );
  }

  const digests = inputs.map((input, index) => {
    switch (wallet.type) {
      case 'p2pkh':
        return tx.hashForSignature(index, wallet.script, SIGHASH_ALL);
      case 'p2wpkh':
      case 'p2sh-p2wpkh':
        // BIP143: the script code of a p2wpkh program is the p2pkh script of its key hash.
        return tx.hashForWitnessV0(
          index,
          outputScript('p2pkh', hash160(wallet.publicKey)),
          input.value,
          SIGHASH_ALL,
        );
      case 'p2tr':
        return tx.hashForWitnessV1(
          index,
          inputs.map(() => wallet.script),
          inputs.map((i) => i.value),
          SIGHASH_DEFAULT,
        );
    }
  });

  let txid: string | undefined;
  if (wallet.type !== 'p2pkh') {
    const final = tx.clone();
    if (wallet.redeemScript) {
      const scriptSig = bitcoin.script.compile([wallet.redeemScript]);
      for (let i = 0; i < inputs.length; i++) final.setInputScript(i, scriptSig);
    }
    txid = final.getId();
  }
  return { psbt: psbt.toBase64(), digests, ...(txid !== undefined ? { txid } : {}) };
}

/** The unsigned transaction a PSBT carries (BIP174 `PSBT_GLOBAL_UNSIGNED_TX`). */
export function unsignedTxOf(psbt: Psbt): Uint8Array {
  return psbt.data.globalMap.unsignedTx.toBuffer();
}

export function parsePsbt(base64: string, network: Network): Psbt {
  // Parsing reads taproot fields and output addresses, which need the ECC backend.
  useNobleEcc();
  try {
    return bitcoin.Psbt.fromBase64(base64, { network });
  } catch {
    throw new ValidationError('INVALID_INTENT', 'the payload is not a valid PSBT');
  }
}

export interface PsbtTxView {
  readonly inputs: readonly {
    readonly outpoint: string;
    readonly txid: string;
    readonly vout: number;
    readonly value: bigint;
    readonly sequence: number;
  }[];
  readonly outputs: readonly { readonly script: Uint8Array; readonly value: bigint }[];
}

/** Inputs (with the values the PSBT commits to) and outputs of our own stored PSBT. */
export function viewPsbt(base64: string, network: Network): PsbtTxView {
  const psbt = parsePsbt(base64, network);
  const inputs = psbt.txInputs.map((input, index) => {
    const data = psbt.data.inputs[index];
    const txid = toHex(Uint8Array.from(input.hash).reverse());
    let value: bigint;
    if (data?.witnessUtxo) value = data.witnessUtxo.value;
    else if (data?.nonWitnessUtxo) {
      const prev = bitcoin.Transaction.fromBuffer(data.nonWitnessUtxo);
      value = (prev.outs[input.index] as { value: bigint }).value;
    } else throw new ValidationError('INVALID_INTENT', 'a PSBT input has no UTXO');
    return {
      outpoint: `${txid}:${input.index}`,
      txid,
      vout: input.index,
      value,
      sequence: input.sequence ?? 0xffffffff,
    };
  });
  const outputs = psbt.txOutputs.map((output) => ({
    script: output.script,
    value: output.value,
  }));
  return { inputs, outputs };
}

/**
 * The signed transaction from our stored PSBT and one signature per request (`in:<i>`):
 * ECDSA as a DER signature with SIGHASH_ALL (partial signature), Schnorr as a 64-byte
 * key-path signature. Nothing but the signature bytes comes from outside.
 */
export function assembleTx(
  base64: string,
  network: Network,
  requests: readonly SigningRequest[],
  signatures: readonly SignatureBundle[],
): { readonly hex: string; readonly txid: string } {
  const psbt = parsePsbt(base64, network);
  if (requests.length !== psbt.inputCount) {
    throw new SigningError('SIGNING_FAILED', 'one signing request per input is required');
  }
  requests.forEach((request, index) => {
    const signature = signatures.find((s) => s.requestId === request.id);
    if (!signature || signature.bytes.length !== 64) {
      throw new SigningError(
        'SIGNING_FAILED',
        `missing signature for request ${request.id}`,
      );
    }
    if (request.scheme === 'secp256k1-schnorr') {
      psbt.updateInput(index, { tapKeySig: signature.bytes });
    } else {
      psbt.updateInput(index, {
        partialSig: [
          {
            pubkey: request.publicKey,
            signature: bitcoin.script.signature.encode(signature.bytes, SIGHASH_ALL),
          },
        ],
      });
    }
  });
  try {
    psbt.finalizeAllInputs();
  } catch {
    throw new SigningError('SIGNING_FAILED', 'the PSBT could not be finalized');
  }
  // The absurd-fee guard ran when the transaction was built; bitcoinjs' own check is off.
  const tx = psbt.extractTransaction(true);
  return { hex: tx.toHex(), txid: tx.getId() };
}

/** Pushes of a script, or `undefined` when it is not push-only. */
function pushes(script: Uint8Array): Uint8Array[] | undefined {
  const chunks = bitcoin.script.decompile(script);
  if (!chunks || chunks.some((c) => typeof c === 'number')) return undefined;
  return chunks as Uint8Array[];
}

/** A serialized witness stack (BIP144): a count, then length-prefixed items. */
function witnessItems(witness: Uint8Array): Uint8Array[] | undefined {
  let offset = 0;
  const varint = (): number | undefined => {
    const first = witness[offset];
    if (first === undefined || first >= 0xfd) return undefined; // no item this long here
    offset += 1;
    return first;
  };
  const count = varint();
  if (count === undefined) return undefined;
  const items: Uint8Array[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const length = varint();
    if (length === undefined || offset + length > witness.length) return undefined;
    items.push(witness.slice(offset, offset + length));
    offset += length;
  }
  return offset === witness.length ? items : undefined;
}

const intentError = (reason: string): ValidationError =>
  new ValidationError('INVALID_INTENT', `the signed PSBT ${reason}`);

/**
 * P3-B (A6): the signatures of a PSBT signed elsewhere. The signed PSBT must carry exactly
 * our unsigned transaction (anti-tamper); from each input only the signature for the
 * request's key is taken (partial or final), and the core then verifies it against the
 * stored digest and key. An input without one is left out (a partial set).
 */
export function signaturesFromPsbt(
  storedBase64: string,
  signedBase64: string,
  network: Network,
  requests: readonly SigningRequest[],
): SignatureBundle[] {
  const stored = parsePsbt(storedBase64, network);
  let signed: Psbt;
  useNobleEcc();
  try {
    signed = bitcoin.Psbt.fromBase64(signedBase64, { network });
  } catch {
    throw intentError('does not decode');
  }
  if (!equalBytes(unsignedTxOf(signed), unsignedTxOf(stored))) {
    throw intentError('is not the prepared transaction');
  }
  const bundles: SignatureBundle[] = [];
  requests.forEach((request, index) => {
    const input = signed.data.inputs[index];
    if (!input) return;
    if (request.scheme === 'secp256k1-schnorr') {
      const final = input.finalScriptWitness
        ? witnessItems(input.finalScriptWitness)
        : undefined;
      const sig = input.tapKeySig ?? (final?.length === 1 ? final[0] : undefined);
      if (!sig) return;
      if (sig.length !== 64)
        throw intentError('uses a sighash type other than SIGHASH_DEFAULT');
      bundles.push({ requestId: request.id, bytes: Uint8Array.from(sig) });
      return;
    }
    let der = input.partialSig?.find((p) =>
      equalBytes(p.pubkey, request.publicKey),
    )?.signature;
    if (!der) {
      const stack = input.finalScriptWitness
        ? witnessItems(input.finalScriptWitness)
        : input.finalScriptSig
          ? pushes(input.finalScriptSig)
          : undefined;
      if (stack?.length === 2 && equalBytes(stack[1] as Uint8Array, request.publicKey)) {
        der = stack[0];
      }
    }
    if (!der) return;
    let decoded: { signature: Uint8Array; hashType: number };
    try {
      decoded = bitcoin.script.signature.decode(der);
    } catch {
      throw intentError('carries a malformed signature');
    }
    if (decoded.hashType !== SIGHASH_ALL)
      throw intentError('uses a sighash type other than SIGHASH_ALL');
    bundles.push({
      requestId: request.id,
      bytes: Uint8Array.from(decoded.signature),
      recovery: recoveryOf(decoded.signature, request),
    });
  });
  return bundles;
}

/** The recovery bit that recovers the request's key (0 when neither does: it then fails verification). */
function recoveryOf(signature: Uint8Array, request: SigningRequest): number {
  for (const bit of [0, 1]) {
    try {
      const key = secp256k1.Signature.fromCompact(signature)
        .addRecoveryBit(bit)
        .recoverPublicKey(request.payload)
        .toRawBytes(true);
      if (equalBytes(key, request.publicKey)) return bit;
    } catch {
      // Try the other bit.
    }
  }
  return 0;
}

/** bitcoinjs network parameters for a network's address parameters. */
export function networkOf(params: {
  bech32: string;
  pubKeyHash: number;
  scriptHash: number;
}): Network {
  const base =
    params.bech32 === 'bc'
      ? bitcoin.networks.bitcoin
      : params.bech32 === 'bcrt'
        ? bitcoin.networks.regtest
        : bitcoin.networks.testnet;
  return {
    ...base,
    bech32: params.bech32,
    pubKeyHash: params.pubKeyHash,
    scriptHash: params.scriptHash,
  };
}

/** The txid of raw transaction bytes (throws on bytes that do not decode). */
export function txidOfHex(hex: string): string {
  return bitcoin.Transaction.fromHex(hex).getId();
}

/**
 * C2: the txid `hex` would have with canonical p2pkh scriptSigs: per input, the last push that
 * is a strict-DER SIGHASH_ALL signature (normalized to low-s) and the last push that is a
 * 33-byte key hashing to `pubkeyHash`, re-pushed minimally as `<sig> <key>`. A third party
 * without the key can change only that encoding (BIP66 strict DER is consensus; the sighash
 * byte is signed), so this equals our Attempt's txid exactly when `hex` is a malleated copy
 * of it: same version, lock time, outpoints, sequences and outputs. `undefined` when an
 * input has a witness or no such pushes.
 */
export function canonicalTwinTxid(
  hex: string,
  pubkeyHash: Uint8Array,
): string | undefined {
  let tx;
  try {
    tx = bitcoin.Transaction.fromHex(hex);
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
  if (tx.hasWitnesses()) return undefined;
  const half = secp256k1.CURVE.n >> 1n;
  for (let index = 0; index < tx.ins.length; index++) {
    const chunks = bitcoin.script.decompile(
      (tx.ins[index] as { script: Uint8Array }).script,
    );
    if (!chunks) return undefined;
    let signature: Uint8Array | undefined;
    let key: Uint8Array | undefined;
    for (const chunk of chunks) {
      if (typeof chunk === 'number') continue;
      if (chunk.length === 33 && equalBytes(hash160(chunk), pubkeyHash)) key = chunk;
      try {
        const decoded = bitcoin.script.signature.decode(chunk);
        if (decoded.hashType === SIGHASH_ALL) signature = decoded.signature;
      } catch {
        // Not a signature push.
      }
    }
    if (!signature || !key) return undefined;
    const r = signature.slice(0, 32);
    let s = bytesToNumberBE(signature.slice(32));
    if (s > half) s = secp256k1.CURVE.n - s;
    const low = concatBytes(r, numberToBytesBE(s, 32));
    tx.setInputScript(
      index,
      bitcoin.script.compile([bitcoin.script.signature.encode(low, SIGHASH_ALL), key]),
    );
  }
  return tx.getId();
}
```

### Task 6: The scripted Esplora node (test-only, ruling A7)

**Files:**
- Create (test support): `test/adapters/utxo/support/node.ts`
- Test: `test/adapters/utxo/node.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `bitcoin`, `Network`, `Transaction` (Task 3 `sdk.ts`); `nobleEcc` (Task 3); `signedSpend`, `signedLegacySpend`, `malleate` (Task 5 `tx.ts`); `FakeClock`, `FakeFetch`, `FakeReply`, `FakeRequest` (`src/testing`); `equalBytes`, `toHex`.
- Produces: `ScriptedEsploraNode` with `new ScriptedEsploraNode({ clock, network?, genesisHash?, minRelayFee?, incrementalRelayFee?, dustRelayFee?, fullRbf?, legacyScriptErrors?, errorFormat? })`, `fetch: FakeFetch`, `options`, `broadcasts: string[]`, `endpoint(name, { lag? }): string` (a base URL), `setLag(name, lag)`, `intercept(name, handler)`, `clearIntercept(name)`, `setFeeEstimates(map)`, `setMempoolMinFee(rate)`, `sendCount(txid)`, `height`, `fund(address, value, { mempool? }): string` (an outpoint), `mine(count?, { skip?, extra? }): string[]`, `reorg(depth, { drop? })`, `evict(txid)`, `inMempool(txid)`, `confirmations(txid)`, `transaction(txid)`, `blockTxids(height)`, `submit(hex): string`; and the Esplora REST routes the driver uses (`/blocks/tip/height`, `/blocks/tip/hash`, `/block-height/:height`, `/block/:hash`, `/block/:hash/txids`, `/block/:hash/txs/:start`, `/tx/:txid`, `/tx/:txid/hex`, `/tx/:txid/status`, `/tx/:txid/outspend/:vout`, `/address/:address`, `/address/:address/utxo`, `/address/:address/txs/chain[/:last_seen_txid]`, `/fee-estimates`, `POST /tx`).
- Defaults model Bitcoin Core 30 (minimum and incremental relay fee 100 sat/kvB, dust relay fee 3,000 sat/kvB, full RBF) and Blockstream's error format.

**Review points (lesson 8, test-double fidelity):**
- `sendrawtransaction` order: already in the UTXO set (−27), already in the mempool (the txid is re-announced, success), then ATMP: `CheckTransaction`, standardness (`tx-size`, `dust`), missing or confirmed-spent inputs (−25 `bad-txns-inputs-missingorspent`), `bad-txns-in-belowout`, `min relay fee not met`, `mempool min fee not met`, the replacement rules, then every signature.
- Replacement rules of Bitcoin Core 28–30: signalling only without full RBF (`txn-mempool-conflict`), at most 100 evictions, no new unconfirmed inputs, a strictly higher rate than each direct conflict, at least the replaced fees, and the incremental relay fee for the new size; the texts are bitcoind's.
- Every input's signature is verified (p2wpkh, p2sh-p2wpkh, p2pkh, p2tr key path) with bitcoinjs' sighash and `@noble/curves`, so a wrong digest in the driver fails like it would on a real node (`block-script-verify-flag-failed`, or `mandatory-script-verify-flag-failed` with `legacyScriptErrors`).
- Esplora semantics the driver relies on: `/tx/:txid` is a 404 for an unknown transaction but `/tx/:txid/status` answers `{ confirmed: false }`; an unknown outpoint is `{ spent: false }`.
- I1, the lag model: an endpoint with lag L hides blocks above `height − L`, but a real lagging Esplora (or a load-balanced backend that is behind) still holds a transaction confirmed above its view **in its mempool**. So `/tx` answers `confirmed: false`, `/outspend` shows the spend unconfirmed, and `/address/.../utxo` treats it as a mempool spend; only a block reward, which never entered a mempool, stays hidden. This is the condition that exercises `includedFinal`'s "found but not final" branch (C1).
- M7, policy as bitcoind applies it: a high-s signature is refused into the mempool (`mempool-script-verify-flag-failed (Non-canonical signature: S value is unnecessarily high)`, or `non-mandatory-script-verify-flag (…)` with `legacyScriptErrors`), as are a scriptSig that is not push-only (`scriptsig-not-pushonly`), a non-minimal push and an unclean stack. Consensus alone runs the scriptSig's last two pushes, so `mine({ extra })`, a miner's block, takes the malleated copies C2 needs. A reorg re-adds disconnected transactions with the fee limits bypassed, as bitcoind does.
- Deterministic: no real timers, `Math.random` or `Date.now`; block times come from the `FakeClock`.

`test/adapters/utxo/node.test.ts`:

```ts
// The scripted Esplora node must model bitcoind's rules exactly (lesson 8): it is what the
// safety tests of the driver and the core run against.
import { walletAddress } from '../../../src/adapters/utxo/address';
import { bitcoin } from '../../../src/adapters/utxo/sdk';
import { FakeClock } from '../../../src/testing/fake-clock';
import { ScriptedEsploraNode, type ScriptedEsploraNodeOptions } from './support/node';
import { malleate, signedLegacySpend, signedSpend, type Malleation } from './support/tx';
import { OTHER_PUBKEY, REGTEST, TEST_KEY, TEST_PUBKEY } from './support/vectors';

const OWN = walletAddress(TEST_PUBKEY, 'p2wpkh', REGTEST);
const PAYEE = walletAddress(OTHER_PUBKEY, 'p2wpkh', REGTEST).script;

function setup(options: Omit<Partial<ScriptedEsploraNodeOptions>, 'clock'> = {}) {
  const node = new ScriptedEsploraNode({ clock: new FakeClock(), ...options });
  const base = node.endpoint('a');
  const [txid] = node.fund(OWN.address, 100_000n).split(':') as [string];
  const get = async (path: string) => {
    const response = await node.fetch.fetch(`${base}${path}`);
    return { status: response.status, text: await response.text() };
  };
  const post = async (hex: string) => {
    const response = await node.fetch.fetch(`${base}/tx`, { method: 'POST', body: hex });
    return { status: response.status, text: await response.text() };
  };
  const spend = (value: bigint, sequence?: number) =>
    signedSpend(TEST_KEY, [[txid, 0, 100_000n]], [[PAYEE, value]], sequence);
  return { node, txid, get, post, spend };
}

const idOf = (hex: string) => bitcoin.Transaction.fromHex(hex).getId();

describe('ScriptedEsploraNode', () => {
  it('serves the chain: genesis, tip, blocks, transactions and unknown ids as Esplora does', async () => {
    const { node, txid, get } = setup();
    expect((await get('/block-height/0')).text).toBe(node.options.genesisHash);
    expect((await get('/blocks/tip/height')).text).toBe('1');
    expect(JSON.parse((await get(`/tx/${txid}`)).text)).toMatchObject({
      txid,
      status: { confirmed: true, block_height: 1 },
    });
    expect(await get(`/tx/${'ab'.repeat(32)}`)).toEqual({
      status: 404,
      text: 'Transaction not found',
    });
    // Esplora's /status answers `confirmed: false` for a transaction it does not know.
    expect(JSON.parse((await get(`/tx/${'ab'.repeat(32)}/status`)).text)).toEqual({
      confirmed: false,
    });
    expect((await get('/block-height/9')).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('accepts a valid spend and re-announces it when sent again', async () => {
    const { node, post, spend } = setup();
    const hex = spend(90_000n);
    expect(await post(hex)).toEqual({ status: 200, text: idOf(hex) });
    expect(await post(hex)).toEqual({ status: 200, text: idOf(hex) });
    node.mine();
    expect(await post(hex)).toEqual({
      status: 400,
      text: 'sendrawtransaction RPC error -27: Transaction outputs already in utxo set',
    });
  });

  it('refuses a spend of an output spent in a block, and junk bytes', async () => {
    const { node, post, spend } = setup();
    await post(spend(90_000n));
    node.mine();
    expect((await post(spend(80_000n))).text).toBe(
      'sendrawtransaction RPC error -25: bad-txns-inputs-missingorspent',
    );
    expect((await post('00')).text).toContain('-22: TX decode failed');
  });

  it('applies the replacement rules of Bitcoin Core 28-30', async () => {
    const { node, post, spend } = setup({ incrementalRelayFee: 1_000n });
    const first = spend(99_000n); // 1,000 sat
    await post(first);
    // A lower absolute fee.
    expect((await post(spend(99_500n))).text).toMatch(
      /-26: insufficient fee, rejecting replacement/,
    );
    // Higher, but not by the incremental relay fee for its size.
    expect((await post(spend(98_990n))).text).toMatch(
      /not enough additional fees to relay/,
    );
    const second = spend(98_000n);
    expect(await post(second)).toEqual({ status: 200, text: idOf(second) });
    expect(node.inMempool(idOf(first))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('honours BIP125 signalling when full RBF is off', async () => {
    const { post, spend } = setup({ fullRbf: false });
    await post(spend(99_000n, 0xffffffff));
    expect((await post(spend(90_000n))).text).toBe(
      'sendrawtransaction RPC error -26: txn-mempool-conflict',
    );
  });

  it('checks the relay fee, dust and every signature, in either error format', async () => {
    const { post, spend, txid } = setup({ errorFormat: 'mempool' });
    expect((await post(spend(99_990n))).text).toBe(
      'sendrawtransaction RPC error: {"code":-26,"message":"min relay fee not met, 10 < 11"}',
    );
    expect((await post(spend(200n))).text).toContain('"message":"dust"');
    const forged = bitcoin.Transaction.fromHex(spend(90_000n));
    const witness = forged.ins[0]!.witness;
    witness[0] = Uint8Array.from(witness[0]!);
    witness[0][10] = witness[0][10]! ^ 1;
    expect((await post(forged.toHex())).text).toContain(
      'block-script-verify-flag-failed',
    );
    void txid;
  });

  it('reorgs blocks back into the mempool, evicts on request and hides blocks from a lagging endpoint', async () => {
    const { node, post, spend, get } = setup();
    const hex = spend(90_000n);
    await post(hex);
    node.mine();
    node.reorg(1);
    expect(node.inMempool(idOf(hex))).toBe(true);
    node.evict(idOf(hex));
    expect(node.inMempool(idOf(hex))).toBe(false);
    node.mine(3);
    node.setLag('a', 2);
    expect((await get('/blocks/tip/height')).text).toBe(String(node.height - 2));
    expect(() => node.reorg(node.height + 1)).toThrow();
  });

  it('shows a transaction confirmed above a lagging view as in its mempool (I1)', async () => {
    const { node, post, spend, get, txid } = setup();
    const hex = spend(90_000n);
    await post(hex);
    node.mine();
    node.setLag('a', 1);
    expect(JSON.parse((await get(`/tx/${idOf(hex)}`)).text).status).toEqual({
      confirmed: false,
    });
    expect(JSON.parse((await get(`/tx/${txid}/outspend/0`)).text)).toEqual({
      spent: true,
      txid: idOf(hex),
      vin: 0,
      status: { confirmed: false },
    });
    // A block reward never entered a mempool: hidden above the view.
    const [coinbase] = node.blockTxids(node.height);
    expect((await get(`/tx/${coinbase}`)).status).toBe(404);
    node.setLag('a', 0);
    expect(JSON.parse((await get(`/tx/${idOf(hex)}`)).text).status).toMatchObject({
      confirmed: true,
    });
  });

  it("refuses non-standard p2pkh signatures into the mempool, but a miner's block takes them (M7)", async () => {
    const { node, post } = setup();
    const legacy = walletAddress(TEST_PUBKEY, 'p2pkh', REGTEST);
    const [txid] = node.fund(legacy.address, 100_000n).split(':') as [string];
    const prev = node.transaction(txid)!.toHex();
    const ours = signedLegacySpend(TEST_KEY, [[txid, 0, prev]], [[PAYEE, 90_000n]]);
    const refusals: [Malleation, string][] = [
      [
        'high-s',
        'mempool-script-verify-flag-failed (Non-canonical signature: S value is unnecessarily high)',
      ],
      [
        'pushdata1',
        'mempool-script-verify-flag-failed (Data push larger than necessary)',
      ],
      [
        'junk-push',
        'mempool-script-verify-flag-failed (Stack size must be exactly one after execution)',
      ],
      ['op-nop', 'scriptsig-not-pushonly'],
    ];
    for (const [kind, text] of refusals) {
      expect((await post(malleate(ours, kind))).text).toBe(
        `sendrawtransaction RPC error -26: ${text}`,
      );
    }
    const copy = malleate(ours, 'high-s');
    node.mine(1, { extra: [copy] });
    expect(node.confirmations(idOf(copy))).toBe(1);
  });

  it('re-adds a disconnected transaction with the fee limits bypassed (M7)', async () => {
    const { node, post, spend } = setup();
    const hex = spend(99_900n); // 100 sat: fine at the default minimum
    await post(hex);
    node.mine();
    node.setMempoolMinFee(50_000n);
    node.reorg(1);
    expect(node.inMempool(idOf(hex))).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the node**

`test/adapters/utxo/support/node.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * A scripted Esplora node for the UTXO family (test-only, ruling A7): an in-memory Bitcoin
 * chain behind Esplora's REST API. It exists to test safety invariants, so it applies
 * bitcoind's rules in bitcoind's order and answers with bitcoind's texts (Plan 3 appendix):
 * decoding and `CheckTransaction`, already-known and already-confirmed, missing or spent
 * inputs, conflicts and the replacement rules of Bitcoin Core 28-30 (full RBF; rules 2-6
 * with an incremental relay fee), standardness (dust, size), the minimum relay fee and
 * consensus script checks (every input signature is verified). Blocks, confirmations,
 * mempool eviction, reorgs and lagging endpoints are scripted by the test. Deterministic:
 * no real timers, `Math.random` or `Date.now`.
 *
 * It decodes and verifies with bitcoinjs-lib and `@noble/curves`, independently of the
 * driver's address codec, so it is not shipped in `crypto-aio/testing`.
 */
import { schnorr, secp256k1 } from '@noble/curves/secp256k1';
import { sha256 } from '@noble/hashes/sha256';
import { utf8ToBytes } from '@noble/hashes/utils';
import { nobleEcc } from '../../../../src/adapters/utxo/ecc';
import {
  bitcoin,
  type Network,
  type Transaction,
} from '../../../../src/adapters/utxo/sdk';
import { equalBytes, toHex } from '../../../../src/core/util/bytes';
import type { FakeClock } from '../../../../src/testing/fake-clock';
import {
  FakeFetch,
  type FakeReply,
  type FakeRequest,
} from '../../../../src/testing/fake-fetch';

const OP_TRUE = Uint8Array.of(0x51);
const PAGE = 25;

export type ErrorFormat = 'blockstream' | 'mempool';

export interface ScriptedEsploraNodeOptions {
  readonly clock: FakeClock;
  readonly network?: Network;
  readonly genesisHash?: string;
  /** sat/kvB; Bitcoin Core v30 defaults. */
  readonly minRelayFee?: bigint;
  readonly incrementalRelayFee?: bigint;
  readonly dustRelayFee?: bigint;
  /** `false`: pre-v28 opt-in RBF (a conflict must signal BIP125). */
  readonly fullRbf?: boolean;
  /** Reject-reason texts of Bitcoin Core before v30 (`mandatory-script-verify-flag-failed`). */
  readonly legacyScriptErrors?: boolean;
  readonly errorFormat?: ErrorFormat;
}

interface Output {
  readonly script: Uint8Array;
  readonly value: bigint;
}

interface Entry {
  readonly tx: Transaction;
  readonly txid: string;
  readonly hex: string;
  readonly fee: bigint;
  readonly vsize: number;
  readonly weight: number;
  /** Funding transactions have no real inputs; Esplora shows them as coinbase. */
  readonly coinbase: boolean;
  readonly prevouts: readonly (Output | undefined)[];
}

interface Block {
  readonly hash: string;
  readonly height: number;
  readonly parentHash: string;
  readonly timestamp: number;
  readonly txids: readonly string[];
}

class NodeError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly code: number,
    readonly text: string,
  ) {
    super(text);
  }
}

const outpointOf = (txid: string, vout: number): string => `${txid}:${vout}`;
const txidOf = (hash: Uint8Array): string => toHex(Uint8Array.from(hash).reverse());
const hex = (bytes: Uint8Array): string => toHex(bytes);

export class ScriptedEsploraNode {
  readonly fetch = new FakeFetch();
  readonly network: Network;
  readonly options: Required<Omit<ScriptedEsploraNodeOptions, 'clock' | 'network'>>;
  /** Raw transactions POSTed to `/tx`, in order (accepted or not). */
  readonly broadcasts: string[] = [];
  readonly #clock: FakeClock;
  readonly #blocks: Block[] = [];
  readonly #txs = new Map<string, Entry>();
  /** Confirmed transactions: txid → height. */
  readonly #confirmed = new Map<string, number>();
  readonly #mempool = new Map<string, Entry>();
  /** outpoint → spending txid (confirmed spends and mempool spends). */
  readonly #spentBy = new Map<string, string>();
  readonly #lag = new Map<string, number>();
  readonly #intercepts = new Map<
    string,
    (request: FakeRequest) => FakeReply | undefined
  >();
  #estimates: Readonly<Record<string, number>> = { '2': 20, '6': 10, '144': 2 };
  /** The dynamic mempool minimum (sat/kvB) a full mempool raises; 0 when not full. */
  #mempoolMinFee = 0n;
  #salt = 0;

  constructor(options: ScriptedEsploraNodeOptions) {
    // bitcoinjs needs an ECC backend for taproot addresses.
    bitcoin.initEccLib(nobleEcc);
    this.#clock = options.clock;
    this.network = options.network ?? bitcoin.networks.regtest;
    this.options = {
      genesisHash:
        options.genesisHash ??
        '0f9188f13cb7b2c71f2a335e3a4fc328bf5beb436012afca590b1a11466e2206',
      minRelayFee: options.minRelayFee ?? 100n,
      incrementalRelayFee: options.incrementalRelayFee ?? 100n,
      dustRelayFee: options.dustRelayFee ?? 3_000n,
      fullRbf: options.fullRbf ?? true,
      legacyScriptErrors: options.legacyScriptErrors ?? false,
      errorFormat: options.errorFormat ?? 'blockstream',
    };
    this.#blocks.push({
      hash: this.options.genesisHash,
      height: 0,
      parentHash: '0'.repeat(64),
      timestamp: Math.floor(this.#clock.now() / 1000),
      txids: [],
    });
  }

  // ---- scripting ------------------------------------------------------------------------

  /** A base URL for an endpoint of this node; `lag` hides that many top blocks from it. */
  endpoint(name: string, options: { readonly lag?: number } = {}): string {
    const base = `https://esplora-${name}.test/api`;
    this.#lag.set(name, options.lag ?? 0);
    this.fetch.route(base, (request) => this.#handle(name, base, request));
    return base;
  }

  setLag(name: string, lag: number): void {
    this.#lag.set(name, lag);
  }

  /** Answers an endpoint's requests with `handler` while it returns a reply. */
  intercept(
    name: string,
    handler: (request: FakeRequest) => FakeReply | undefined,
  ): void {
    this.#intercepts.set(name, handler);
  }

  clearIntercept(name: string): void {
    this.#intercepts.delete(name);
  }

  setFeeEstimates(estimates: Readonly<Record<string, number>>): void {
    this.#estimates = estimates;
  }

  /** A full mempool's minimum fee rate (sat/kvB): lower transactions are refused. */
  setMempoolMinFee(rate: bigint): void {
    this.#mempoolMinFee = rate;
  }

  /** How many times these exact transaction bytes (by txid) were POSTed. */
  sendCount(txid: string): number {
    return this.broadcasts.filter((hex) => {
      try {
        return bitcoin.Transaction.fromHex(hex).getId() === txid;
      } catch {
        return false;
      }
    }).length;
  }

  get height(): number {
    return this.#blocks.length - 1;
  }

  /**
   * Pays `value` to `address` in a funding transaction (shown as a coinbase), confirmed in
   * a new block unless `mempool`. Returns its outpoint.
   */
  fund(
    address: string,
    value: bigint,
    options: { readonly mempool?: boolean } = {},
  ): string {
    const tx = new bitcoin.Transaction();
    tx.version = 2;
    tx.addInput(
      sha256(utf8ToBytes(`fund:${this.#salt++}`)),
      0xffffffff,
      0xffffffff,
      OP_TRUE,
    );
    tx.addOutput(bitcoin.address.toOutputScript(address, this.network), value);
    const entry = this.#entry(tx, true);
    this.#txs.set(entry.txid, entry);
    if (options.mempool) this.#toMempool(entry);
    else this.mine(1, { extra: [entry] });
    return outpointOf(entry.txid, 0);
  }

  /**
   * Mines `count` blocks. The first includes every mempool transaction (parents first)
   * except `skip`, plus `extra` transactions that never went through the mempool (a
   * miner's own double spend, which evicts what it conflicts with).
   */
  mine(
    count = 1,
    options: {
      readonly skip?: readonly string[];
      readonly extra?: readonly (Entry | string)[];
    } = {},
  ): string[] {
    const hashes: string[] = [];
    for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
      const included: Entry[] = [];
      if (n === 0) {
        for (const item of options.extra ?? []) {
          const entry =
            typeof item === 'string'
              ? this.#entry(bitcoin.Transaction.fromHex(item), false)
              : item;
          this.#txs.set(entry.txid, entry);
          this.#evictConflicts(entry);
          included.push(entry);
        }
        const skip = new Set(options.skip ?? []);
        for (const txid of [...skip])
          for (const d of this.#descendants(txid)) skip.add(d);
        const pending = [...this.#mempool.values()].filter((e) => !skip.has(e.txid));
        const done = new Set(included.map((e) => e.txid));
        while (pending.length > 0) {
          const index = pending.findIndex((e) =>
            e.tx.ins.every((input) => {
              const parent = txidOf(input.hash);
              return !this.#mempool.has(parent) || done.has(parent);
            }),
          );
          if (index < 0) break;
          const [entry] = pending.splice(index, 1) as [Entry];
          done.add(entry.txid);
          included.push(entry);
        }
      }
      const parent = this.#blocks[this.#blocks.length - 1] as Block;
      const height = parent.height + 1;
      const hash = hex(sha256(utf8ToBytes(`${parent.hash}:${height}:${this.#salt++}`)));
      const coinbase = new bitcoin.Transaction();
      coinbase.version = 2;
      coinbase.addInput(
        new Uint8Array(32),
        0xffffffff,
        0xffffffff,
        Uint8Array.of(0x01, height & 0xff, 0x51),
      );
      coinbase.addOutput(
        OP_TRUE,
        included.reduce((sum, e) => sum + e.fee, 0n),
      );
      const cb = this.#entry(coinbase, true);
      this.#txs.set(cb.txid, cb);
      const block: Block = {
        hash,
        height,
        parentHash: parent.hash,
        timestamp: Math.floor(this.#clock.now() / 1000),
        txids: [cb.txid, ...included.map((e) => e.txid)],
      };
      this.#blocks.push(block);
      for (const txid of block.txids) {
        const entry = this.#txs.get(txid) as Entry;
        this.#mempool.delete(txid);
        this.#confirmed.set(txid, height);
        if (!entry.coinbase)
          entry.tx.ins.forEach((input) =>
            this.#spentBy.set(outpointOf(txidOf(input.hash), input.index), txid),
          );
      }
      hashes.push(hash);
    }
    return hashes;
  }

  /**
   * Disconnects the top `depth` blocks, as bitcoind does in a reorg: their transactions go
   * back to the mempool (parents first) unless listed in `drop`; call `mine` for the new
   * branch. Refuses to disconnect the genesis block.
   */
  reorg(depth: number, options: { readonly drop?: readonly string[] } = {}): void {
    if (!Number.isInteger(depth) || depth < 1 || depth > this.height) {
      throw new Error(`cannot reorg ${depth} blocks at height ${this.height}`);
    }
    const drop = new Set(options.drop ?? []);
    const returned: Entry[] = [];
    for (let n = 0; n < depth; n++) {
      const block = this.#blocks.pop() as Block;
      for (const txid of [...block.txids].reverse()) {
        const entry = this.#txs.get(txid) as Entry;
        this.#confirmed.delete(txid);
        if (!entry.coinbase) {
          entry.tx.ins.forEach((input) =>
            this.#spentBy.delete(outpointOf(txidOf(input.hash), input.index)),
          );
        }
        if (!entry.coinbase && !drop.has(txid)) returned.unshift(entry);
      }
    }
    for (const entry of returned) {
      try {
        this.#accept(entry, true);
      } catch {
        // bitcoind drops a disconnected transaction that is no longer valid.
      }
    }
  }

  /** Mempool eviction or expiry (`-mempoolexpiry`, a full mempool): the tx and its descendants go. */
  evict(txid: string): void {
    for (const d of [txid, ...this.#descendants(txid)]) this.#removeFromMempool(d);
  }

  inMempool(txid: string): boolean {
    return this.#mempool.has(txid);
  }

  confirmations(txid: string): number {
    const height = this.#confirmed.get(txid);
    return height === undefined ? 0 : this.height - height + 1;
  }

  /** The txids of the block at `height` (its coinbase first), for assertions. */
  blockTxids(height: number): readonly string[] {
    return this.#blocks[height]?.txids ?? [];
  }

  /** The decoded transaction by id (any state), for assertions. */
  transaction(txid: string): Transaction | undefined {
    return this.#txs.get(txid)?.tx;
  }

  /** Accepts raw bytes as `POST /tx` does; throws the node error text on refusal. */
  submit(hex: string): string {
    return this.#submit(hex);
  }

  // ---- mempool acceptance (bitcoind's AcceptToMemoryPool order) ----------------------------

  #entry(tx: Transaction, coinbase: boolean): Entry {
    const prevouts = coinbase
      ? tx.ins.map(() => undefined)
      : tx.ins.map((input) => this.#output(txidOf(input.hash), input.index));
    const inValue = prevouts.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p?.value ?? 0n), 0n);
    const outValue = tx.outs.reduce((sum, o) => sum + o.value, 0n);
    const weight = tx.weight();
    return {
      tx,
      txid: tx.getId(),
      hex: tx.toHex(),
      fee: coinbase ? 0n : inValue - outValue,
      vsize: Math.ceil(weight / 4),
      weight,
      coinbase,
      prevouts,
    };
  }

  #output(txid: string, vout: number): Output | undefined {
    const out = this.#txs.get(txid)?.tx.outs[vout];
    return out ? { script: out.script, value: out.value } : undefined;
  }

  /** `sendrawtransaction` (node/transaction.cpp `BroadcastTransaction`), then ATMP. */
  #submit(hexTx: string): string {
    let tx: Transaction;
    try {
      tx = bitcoin.Transaction.fromHex(hexTx.trim());
    } catch {
      throw new NodeError(
        -22,
        'TX decode failed. Make sure the tx has at least one input.',
      );
    }
    const txid = tx.getId();
    if (this.#confirmed.has(txid)) {
      const unspent = tx.outs.some(
        (_, vout) => !this.#spentBy.has(outpointOf(txid, vout)),
      );
      if (unspent) throw new NodeError(-27, 'Transaction outputs already in utxo set');
    }
    // Already in the mempool (any witness): bitcoind re-announces it and returns the txid.
    if (this.#mempool.has(txid)) return txid;
    this.#checkTransaction(tx);
    const entry = this.#entry(tx, false);
    this.#accept(entry);
    this.#txs.set(txid, entry);
    return txid;
  }

  #checkTransaction(tx: Transaction): void {
    if (tx.ins.length === 0) throw new NodeError(-26, 'bad-txns-vin-empty');
    if (tx.outs.length === 0) throw new NodeError(-26, 'bad-txns-vout-empty');
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const input of tx.ins) {
      const key = outpointOf(txidOf(input.hash), input.index);
      if (seen.has(key)) throw new NodeError(-26, 'bad-txns-inputs-duplicate');
      seen.add(key);
    }
    for (const output of tx.outs) {
      if (output.value > 2_100_000_000_000_000n)
        throw new NodeError(-26, 'bad-txns-vout-toolarge');
    }
  }

  /**
   * ATMP's order: standardness, inputs, values, the relay fee, replacement, then scripts.
   * `reorg`: a transaction of a disconnected block, re-added with fee limits bypassed (M7).
   */
  #accept(entry: Entry, reorg = false): void {
    const { tx } = entry;
    if (entry.weight > 400_000) throw new NodeError(-26, 'tx-size');
    for (const output of tx.outs) {
      if (output.value < this.#dust(output.script)) throw new NodeError(-26, 'dust');
    }
    const conflicts = new Set<string>();
    tx.ins.forEach((input, index) => {
      const parent = txidOf(input.hash);
      const spender = this.#spentBy.get(outpointOf(parent, input.index));
      const parentLive = this.#confirmed.has(parent) || this.#mempool.has(parent);
      if (
        !entry.prevouts[index] ||
        !parentLive ||
        (spender !== undefined && this.#confirmed.has(spender))
      ) {
        throw new NodeError(-25, 'bad-txns-inputs-missingorspent');
      }
      if (spender !== undefined && this.#mempool.has(spender)) conflicts.add(spender);
    });
    const inValue = entry.prevouts.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p?.value ?? 0n), 0n);
    const outValue = tx.outs.reduce((sum, o) => sum + o.value, 0n);
    if (inValue < outValue) {
      throw new NodeError(
        -26,
        `bad-txns-in-belowout, value in (${inValue}) < value out (${outValue})`,
      );
    }
    const minFee = (this.options.minRelayFee * BigInt(entry.vsize) + 999n) / 1000n;
    if (!reorg && entry.fee < minFee) {
      throw new NodeError(-26, `min relay fee not met, ${entry.fee} < ${minFee}`);
    }
    const poolFee = (this.#mempoolMinFee * BigInt(entry.vsize) + 999n) / 1000n;
    if (!reorg && entry.fee < poolFee) {
      throw new NodeError(-26, `mempool min fee not met, ${entry.fee} < ${poolFee}`);
    }
    if (conflicts.size > 0) this.#checkReplacement(entry, conflicts);
    this.#checkScripts(entry);
    for (const conflict of conflicts) this.evict(conflict);
    this.#toMempool(entry);
  }

  #toMempool(entry: Entry): void {
    this.#mempool.set(entry.txid, entry);
    if (!entry.coinbase) {
      entry.tx.ins.forEach((input) =>
        this.#spentBy.set(outpointOf(txidOf(input.hash), input.index), entry.txid),
      );
    }
  }

  #removeFromMempool(txid: string): void {
    const entry = this.#mempool.get(txid);
    if (!entry) return;
    this.#mempool.delete(txid);
    entry.tx.ins.forEach((input) => {
      const key = outpointOf(txidOf(input.hash), input.index);
      if (this.#spentBy.get(key) === txid) this.#spentBy.delete(key);
    });
  }

  #descendants(txid: string): string[] {
    const out: string[] = [];
    const queue = [txid];
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const current = queue.shift() as string;
      for (const entry of this.#mempool.values()) {
        if (out.includes(entry.txid)) continue;
        if (entry.tx.ins.some((input) => txidOf(input.hash) === current)) {
          out.push(entry.txid);
          queue.push(entry.txid);
        }
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  #evictConflicts(entry: Entry): void {
    for (const input of entry.tx.ins) {
      const spender = this.#spentBy.get(outpointOf(txidOf(input.hash), input.index));
      if (spender !== undefined && this.#mempool.has(spender)) this.evict(spender);
    }
  }

  /** Bitcoin Core 28-30 replacement policy (rules 2-6; signalling only without full RBF). */
  #checkReplacement(entry: Entry, conflicts: ReadonlySet<string>): void {
    const originals = new Set<string>();
    for (const txid of conflicts) {
      const original = this.#mempool.get(txid) as Entry;
      if (
        !this.options.fullRbf &&
        !original.tx.ins.some((i) => i.sequence < 0xfffffffe)
      ) {
        throw new NodeError(-26, 'txn-mempool-conflict');
      }
      originals.add(txid);
      for (const d of this.#descendants(txid)) originals.add(d);
    }
    if (originals.size > 100) {
      throw new NodeError(
        -26,
        `too many potential replacements, rejecting replacement ${entry.txid}; too many potential replacements (${originals.size} > 100)`,
      );
    }
    // Rule 2: no new unconfirmed inputs.
    const originalParents = new Set(
      [...conflicts].flatMap((txid) =>
        (this.#mempool.get(txid) as Entry).tx.ins.map((i) => txidOf(i.hash)),
      ),
    );
    for (const input of entry.tx.ins) {
      const parent = txidOf(input.hash);
      if (this.#mempool.has(parent) && !originalParents.has(parent)) {
        throw new NodeError(
          -26,
          `replacement-adds-unconfirmed, replacement ${entry.txid} adds unconfirmed input, idx 0`,
        );
      }
    }
    // Rule 6 (pre-v31): a strictly higher fee rate than each directly conflicting transaction.
    for (const txid of conflicts) {
      const original = this.#mempool.get(txid) as Entry;
      if (entry.fee * BigInt(original.vsize) <= original.fee * BigInt(entry.vsize)) {
        throw new NodeError(
          -26,
          `insufficient fee, rejecting replacement ${entry.txid}; new feerate ${entry.fee} <= old feerate ${original.fee}`,
        );
      }
    }
    // Rules 3 and 4.
    const paid = [...originals].reduce(
      (sum, txid) => sum + (this.#mempool.get(txid) as Entry).fee,
      0n,
    );
    if (entry.fee < paid) {
      throw new NodeError(
        -26,
        `insufficient fee, rejecting replacement ${entry.txid}, less fees than conflicting txs; ${entry.fee} < ${paid}`,
      );
    }
    const relay = (this.options.incrementalRelayFee * BigInt(entry.vsize) + 999n) / 1000n;
    if (entry.fee - paid < relay) {
      throw new NodeError(
        -26,
        `insufficient fee, rejecting replacement ${entry.txid}, not enough additional fees to relay; ${entry.fee - paid} < ${relay}`,
      );
    }
  }

  #dust(script: Uint8Array): bigint {
    if (script[0] === 0x6a) return 0n; // OP_RETURN is unspendable
    const size = BigInt(8 + 1 + script.length);
    const witness =
      (script[0] === 0x00 || (script[0]! >= 0x51 && script[0]! <= 0x60)) &&
      script.length >= 4 &&
      script.length <= 42 &&
      script[1] === script.length - 2;
    return ((size + (witness ? 67n : 148n)) * this.options.dustRelayFee + 999n) / 1000n;
  }

  /** Consensus script checks for the four wallet types (and OP_TRUE test outputs). */
  /** Consensus script checks for the four wallet types, then bitcoind's policy flags (M7). */
  #checkScripts(entry: Entry): void {
    const { tx, prevouts } = entry;
    const policyFail = (why: string): never => {
      throw new NodeError(
        -26,
        this.options.legacyScriptErrors
          ? `non-mandatory-script-verify-flag (${why})`
          : `mempool-script-verify-flag-failed (${why})`,
      );
    };
    const half = secp256k1.CURVE.n >> 1n;
    const scripts = prevouts.map((p) => (p as Output).script);
    const values = prevouts.map((p) => (p as Output).value);
    const fail = (): never => {
      throw new NodeError(
        -26,
        this.options.legacyScriptErrors
          ? 'mandatory-script-verify-flag-failed (Signature must be zero for failed CHECK(MULTI)SIG operation)'
          : 'block-script-verify-flag-failed (Signature must be zero for failed CHECK(MULTI)SIG operation)',
      );
    };
    tx.ins.forEach((input, index) => {
      const script = scripts[index] as Uint8Array;
      const value = values[index] as bigint;
      const ecdsa = (
        sig: Uint8Array,
        pubkey: Uint8Array,
        digestFor: (hashType: number) => Uint8Array,
      ) => {
        let decoded;
        try {
          decoded = bitcoin.script.signature.decode(sig);
        } catch {
          return fail();
        }
        const digest = digestFor(decoded.hashType);
        if (!secp256k1.verify(decoded.signature, digest, pubkey, { lowS: false })) fail();
        const s = BigInt(`0x${hex(decoded.signature.slice(32))}`);
        if (s > half)
          policyFail('Non-canonical signature: S value is unnecessarily high');
      };
      const hash160 = (data: Uint8Array) => bitcoin.crypto.hash160(data);
      if (equalBytes(script, OP_TRUE)) return;
      if (script.length === 22 && script[0] === 0x00 && script[1] === 0x14) {
        const [sig, pubkey] = input.witness;
        if (!sig || !pubkey || !equalBytes(hash160(pubkey), script.slice(2)))
          return fail();
        const code = bitcoin.payments.p2pkh({ hash: script.slice(2) })
          .output as Uint8Array;
        return ecdsa(sig, pubkey, (t) => tx.hashForWitnessV0(index, code, value, t));
      }
      if (script.length === 23 && script[0] === 0xa9) {
        const chunks = bitcoin.script.decompile(input.script) ?? [];
        const redeem = chunks[0];
        if (
          chunks.length !== 1 ||
          !(redeem instanceof Uint8Array) ||
          !equalBytes(hash160(redeem), script.slice(2, 22))
        )
          return fail();
        const [sig, pubkey] = input.witness;
        if (
          redeem.length !== 22 ||
          !sig ||
          !pubkey ||
          !equalBytes(hash160(pubkey), redeem.slice(2))
        )
          return fail();
        const code = bitcoin.payments.p2pkh({ hash: redeem.slice(2) })
          .output as Uint8Array;
        return ecdsa(sig, pubkey, (t) => tx.hashForWitnessV0(index, code, value, t));
      }
      if (script.length === 25 && script[0] === 0x76) {
        // Consensus runs the scriptSig: the last two pushes meet OP_CHECKSIG.
        const chunks = bitcoin.script.decompile(input.script) ?? [];
        const pushes = chunks.filter((c): c is Uint8Array => c instanceof Uint8Array);
        const [sig, pubkey] = pushes.slice(-2);
        if (!sig || !pubkey || !equalBytes(hash160(pubkey), script.slice(3, 23))) {
          return fail();
        }
        ecdsa(sig, pubkey, (t) => tx.hashForSignature(index, script, t));
        // Policy: push-only, a clean stack and minimal pushes.
        if (chunks.length !== pushes.length)
          throw new NodeError(-26, 'scriptsig-not-pushonly');
        if (chunks.length !== 2)
          policyFail('Stack size must be exactly one after execution');
        if (!equalBytes(input.script, bitcoin.script.compile([sig, pubkey]))) {
          policyFail('Data push larger than necessary');
        }
        return;
      }
      if (script.length === 34 && script[0] === 0x51 && script[1] === 0x20) {
        const [sig] = input.witness;
        if (
          !sig ||
          input.witness.length !== 1 ||
          (sig.length !== 64 && sig.length !== 65)
        )
          return fail();
        const hashType = sig.length === 65 ? (sig[64] as number) : 0x00;
        const digest = tx.hashForWitnessV1(index, scripts, values, hashType);
        if (!schnorr.verify(sig.slice(0, 64), digest, script.slice(2))) fail();
        return;
      }
      throw new NodeError(-26, 'bad-txns-nonstandard-inputs');
    });
  }

  // ---- the Esplora REST API ---------------------------------------------------------------

  #viewHeight(endpoint: string): number {
    return Math.max(0, this.height - (this.#lag.get(endpoint) ?? 0));
  }

  #status(txid: string, endpoint: string): Record<string, unknown> {
    const height = this.#confirmed.get(txid);
    if (height === undefined || height > this.#viewHeight(endpoint))
      return { confirmed: false };
    const block = this.#blocks[height] as Block;
    return {
      confirmed: true,
      block_height: height,
      block_hash: block.hash,
      block_time: block.timestamp,
    };
  }

  /**
   * I1: a lagging Esplora (or a load-balanced backend that is behind) still holds a
   * transaction confirmed above its view in its mempool, so it shows it unconfirmed; only
   * a block reward never entered a mempool and stays hidden.
   */
  #visible(txid: string, endpoint: string): boolean {
    if (this.#mempool.has(txid)) return true;
    const height = this.#confirmed.get(txid);
    if (height === undefined) return false;
    return (
      height <= this.#viewHeight(endpoint) || !(this.#txs.get(txid) as Entry).coinbase
    );
  }

  #outputJson(output: Output): Record<string, unknown> {
    let address: string | undefined;
    try {
      address = bitcoin.address.fromOutputScript(output.script, this.network);
    } catch {
      address = undefined;
    }
    const s = output.script;
    const type =
      s.length === 22 && s[0] === 0x00
        ? 'v0_p2wpkh'
        : s.length === 34 && s[0] === 0x00
          ? 'v0_p2wsh'
          : s.length === 34 && s[0] === 0x51
            ? 'v1_p2tr'
            : s.length === 25 && s[0] === 0x76
              ? 'p2pkh'
              : s.length === 23 && s[0] === 0xa9
                ? 'p2sh'
                : s[0] === 0x6a
                  ? 'op_return'
                  : 'unknown';
    return {
      scriptpubkey: hex(s),
      scriptpubkey_asm: '',
      scriptpubkey_type: type,
      ...(address !== undefined ? { scriptpubkey_address: address } : {}),
      value: Number(output.value),
    };
  }

  #txJson(entry: Entry, endpoint: string): Record<string, unknown> {
    return {
      txid: entry.txid,
      version: entry.tx.version,
      locktime: entry.tx.locktime,
      vin: entry.tx.ins.map((input, index) =>
        entry.coinbase
          ? {
              txid: '0'.repeat(64),
              vout: 0xffffffff,
              prevout: null,
              scriptsig: hex(input.script),
              witness: [],
              is_coinbase: true,
              sequence: input.sequence,
            }
          : {
              txid: txidOf(input.hash),
              vout: input.index,
              prevout: this.#outputJson(entry.prevouts[index] as Output),
              scriptsig: hex(input.script),
              witness: input.witness.map(hex),
              is_coinbase: false,
              sequence: input.sequence,
            },
      ),
      vout: entry.tx.outs.map((o) => this.#outputJson(o)),
      size: entry.tx.byteLength(),
      weight: entry.weight,
      fee: Number(entry.fee),
      status: this.#status(entry.txid, endpoint),
    };
  }

  #script(address: string): Uint8Array | undefined {
    try {
      return bitcoin.address.toOutputScript(address, this.network);
    } catch {
      return undefined;
    }
  }

  #utxos(script: Uint8Array, endpoint: string): Record<string, unknown>[] {
    const out: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
    for (const entry of this.#txs.values()) {
      if (!this.#visible(entry.txid, endpoint)) continue;
      entry.tx.outs.forEach((output, vout) => {
        if (!equalBytes(output.script, script)) return;
        const spender = this.#spentBy.get(outpointOf(entry.txid, vout));
        if (spender !== undefined && this.#visible(spender, endpoint)) return;
        out.push({
          txid: entry.txid,
          vout,
          status: this.#status(entry.txid, endpoint),
          value: Number(output.value),
        });
      });
    }
    return out;
  }

  #touches(entry: Entry, script: Uint8Array): boolean {
    return (
      entry.tx.outs.some((o) => equalBytes(o.script, script)) ||
      entry.prevouts.some((p) => p !== undefined && equalBytes(p.script, script))
    );
  }

  #handle(endpoint: string, base: string, request: FakeRequest): FakeReply {
    const intercepted = this.#intercepts.get(endpoint)?.(request);
    if (intercepted) return intercepted;
    const path = request.url.href.slice(base.length).split('?')[0] as string;
    const parts = path.split('/').filter(Boolean);
    const notFound = (text: string): FakeReply => ({ status: 404, text });
    const view = this.#viewHeight(endpoint);
    if (request.method === 'POST' && path === '/tx') {
      const body = request.body ?? '';
      this.broadcasts.push(body);
      try {
        return { text: this.#submit(body) };
      } catch (error) {
        if (!(error instanceof NodeError)) throw error;
        const text =
          this.options.errorFormat === 'mempool'
            ? `sendrawtransaction RPC error: ${JSON.stringify({ code: error.code, message: error.text })}`
            : `sendrawtransaction RPC error ${error.code}: ${error.text}`;
        return { status: 400, text };
      }
    }
    if (request.method !== 'GET') return { status: 405, text: 'Invalid method' };
    const [head, a, b, c] = parts;
    if (head === 'blocks' && a === 'tip' && b === 'height') return { text: String(view) };
    if (head === 'blocks' && a === 'tip' && b === 'hash')
      return { text: (this.#blocks[view] as Block).hash };
    if (head === 'block-height' && a !== undefined) {
      const block = this.#blocks[Number(a)];
      return block && block.height <= view
        ? { text: block.hash }
        : notFound('Block not found');
    }
    if (head === 'block' && a !== undefined) {
      const block = this.#blocks.find((x) => x.hash === a && x.height <= view);
      if (!block) return notFound('Block not found');
      if (b === undefined) {
        return {
          json: {
            id: block.hash,
            height: block.height,
            version: 0x20000000,
            timestamp: block.timestamp,
            tx_count: block.txids.length,
            size: 1000,
            weight: 4000,
            merkle_root: '0'.repeat(64),
            previousblockhash: block.height === 0 ? null : block.parentHash,
            mediantime: block.timestamp,
            nonce: 0,
            bits: 0x207fffff,
            difficulty: 1,
          },
        };
      }
      if (b === 'txids') return { json: block.txids };
      if (b === 'txs') {
        const start = Number(c ?? 0);
        if (!Number.isInteger(start) || start % PAGE !== 0)
          return { status: 400, text: 'start index must be a multipication of 25' };
        return {
          json: block.txids
            .slice(start, start + PAGE)
            .map((id) => this.#txJson(this.#txs.get(id) as Entry, endpoint)),
        };
      }
    }
    if (head === 'tx' && a !== undefined) {
      const entry = this.#txs.get(a);
      if (b === 'status')
        return {
          json:
            entry && this.#visible(a, endpoint)
              ? this.#status(a, endpoint)
              : { confirmed: false },
        };
      if (b === 'outspend' && c !== undefined) {
        const spender = this.#spentBy.get(outpointOf(a, Number(c)));
        if (spender === undefined || !this.#visible(spender, endpoint))
          return { json: { spent: false } };
        const vin = (this.#txs.get(spender) as Entry).tx.ins.findIndex(
          (i) => txidOf(i.hash) === a && i.index === Number(c),
        );
        return {
          json: {
            spent: true,
            txid: spender,
            vin,
            status: this.#status(spender, endpoint),
          },
        };
      }
      if (!entry || !this.#visible(a, endpoint)) return notFound('Transaction not found');
      if (b === undefined) return { json: this.#txJson(entry, endpoint) };
      if (b === 'hex') return { text: entry.hex };
    }
    if (head === 'address' && a !== undefined) {
      const script = this.#script(a);
      if (!script) return { status: 400, text: 'Invalid Bitcoin address' };
      if (b === 'utxo') return { json: this.#utxos(script, endpoint) };
      if (b === undefined) {
        let funded = 0n;
        let spent = 0n;
        for (const entry of this.#txs.values()) {
          const height = this.#confirmed.get(entry.txid);
          if (height === undefined || height > view) continue;
          entry.tx.outs.forEach((o) => {
            if (equalBytes(o.script, script)) funded += o.value;
          });
          entry.prevouts.forEach((p) => {
            if (p && equalBytes(p.script, script)) spent += p.value;
          });
        }
        return {
          json: {
            address: a,
            chain_stats: {
              funded_txo_count: 0,
              funded_txo_sum: Number(funded),
              spent_txo_count: 0,
              spent_txo_sum: Number(spent),
              tx_count: 0,
            },
            mempool_stats: {
              funded_txo_count: 0,
              funded_txo_sum: 0,
              spent_txo_count: 0,
              spent_txo_sum: 0,
              tx_count: 0,
            },
          },
        };
      }
      if (b === 'txs' && c === 'chain') {
        const lastSeen = parts[4];
        const history: Entry[] = [];
        for (let h = view; h >= 0; h--) {
          for (const txid of [...(this.#blocks[h] as Block).txids].reverse()) {
            const entry = this.#txs.get(txid) as Entry;
            if (this.#touches(entry, script)) history.push(entry);
          }
        }
        const from = lastSeen ? history.findIndex((e) => e.txid === lastSeen) + 1 : 0;
        if (lastSeen && from === 0) return { json: [] };
        return {
          json: history.slice(from, from + PAGE).map((e) => this.#txJson(e, endpoint)),
        };
      }
    }
    if (head === 'fee-estimates') return { json: this.#estimates };
    return notFound('not found');
  }
}
```

### Task 7: The Esplora client, transaction decoding and the reader

**Files:**
- Create: `src/adapters/utxo/esplora.ts`, `src/adapters/utxo/context.ts`, `src/adapters/utxo/decode.ts`, `src/adapters/utxo/reader.ts`
- Create (test support): `test/adapters/utxo/support/harness.ts`
- Test: `test/adapters/utxo/reader.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Esplora wire types, `UtxoCallTags`, `UtxoOutputType`, `UtxoUnspent`, `UtxoNetworkConfig`, `utxoNetworkConfig`, `BITCOIN_CHAIN` (Task 2); `decodeAddress`, `walletAddress`, `AddressParams`, `DecodedAddress` (Task 3); `networkOf` (Task 5, in the harness), `canonicalTwinTxid`, `txidOfHex` (Task 5); `ScriptedEsploraNode` (Task 6); `Transport`, `HttpRequest`, `HttpTransport`, `EventBus`, the driver port types, `ProviderError`, `ConfigError`, `UnsupportedCapabilityError`, `ValidationError`, `isCryptoAioError` (core).
- Produces:
  - `esplora.ts`: `EsploraClient(chain: Transport, indexer: Transport)` with `tipHeight`, `blockHashAt` (404 → `null`), `block`, `blockTxids`, `blockTxs`, `tx` (404 → `null`), `txHex`, `outspend`, `feeEstimates`, `broadcast`, `addressUtxos`, `addressStats`, `addressTxs`, each taking `UtxoCallTags` (a caller `quorumKey` included, lesson 17); the parsers `parseHeight`, `parseHash`, `parseStatus`, `parseTx`, `parseOutspend`, `parseFeeEstimates`; `malformed(what)`, `isNotFound(error)`, `isHash(value)`. `tx` and `block` refuse an answer for another id as `malformed` (I2), and every caller id or address that goes into a URL path must be exactly 64 lowercase hex digits (`INVALID_INTENT`) or an alphanumeric address (`INVALID_ADDRESS`).
  - `context.ts`: `UtxoContext { esplora; chain; network; config; log }`, `READ`, `MONITOR`, `PROOF`, `withSignal(tags, signal?)`, `proofRead(read)` (lesson 18, widened), `ADDRESS_TYPES`, `ParsedWalletOptions`, `parseWalletOptions(wallet, config)` (`CONFIG_INVALID` for an unknown option, a bad type, an undecodable `changeAddress` or a non-boolean `allowExternalChangeAddress`), `assertNative(asset)` (`UNSUPPORTED_CAPABILITY`), `parseOutpoint(outpoint)` (a malformed `txid:vout` is a retryable `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`).
  - `decode.ts`: `observationOf(txid, status): DriverTxObservation`, `decodeTransaction(tx, params): DriverTransaction`.
  - `reader.ts`: `addressCodec(ctx): AddressCodec`, `finalizedFromHead(ctx, head): bigint`, `chainReader(ctx): ChainReader`, `addressHistory(ctx): AddressHistorySource`, `listUnspent(ctx, address): Promise<UtxoUnspent[]>`, `isOwnCopy(ctx, refId, from, spender, tags): Promise<boolean>` (C2, shared with Task 9's proofs).
  - Test support `harness.ts`: `REGTEST_NETWORK`, `RecordedCall`, `HarnessOptions { endpoints?; node?; options? }`, `utxoHarness(options?)` returning `{ clock, node, ctx, network, transport, indexer, calls, run, options }`, `Harness`.

**Review points:**
- R41/R14: reads are `read`, heights and observations `monitor`, address reads go to the indexer transport, every call has an identifier-free `route`; no quorum on reads.
- Lesson 6: every Esplora answer is parsed strictly (hex ids, safe-integer satoshis up to 21M BTC, statuses); anything malformed is a retryable `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, never a foreign error; a 2xx broadcast answer that is not a txid is ambiguous (the node may have taken the bytes).
- D17: `observe` reads `/tx/:txid` (404 = not visible), never `/status`, which says `confirmed: false` for an unknown transaction.
- D18/lesson 15: decoding reports the chain as it is (`vout:<n>` transfers from the input addresses; `partial` for an unnamed output with value).
- A 404 is `null` only when it is definitive (`RPC_ERROR`, not ambiguous); every other failure propagates unchanged.
- I2, answers bound to requests: `/tx/:txid` must answer that txid and `/block/:hash` that hash, else `malformed` (retryable `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`), so one faulty endpoint cannot give our Attempt an observed `included` with a foreign `txHash`. `getTransaction`, `getBlock(string)` and `observe` lowercase the id and require 64 hex digits first: a malformed id is `null` (reads) or `{ seen: 'none' }` (observe) and never reaches a URL path, where `..` would resolve to another route.
- C2 in `observe`: when `/tx/:ref` is a 404, the sender is p2pkh and the ordering is present, the first input's spender is read (`MONITOR`); if `isOwnCopy` holds, the observation is the copy's (`observationOf(copy, status)`: `mempool` or `included`, never `replaced`). This is observed evidence only; the terminal verdict comes from Task 9's quorum path. For a segwit or taproot sender nothing extra is read (their txids exclude the witness).
- Lesson 18, widened (R85): on a proof path only a definitive negative proof may answer "no"; every other RPC or HTTP error decides nothing. `proofRead` turns every non-retryable provider error (a CDN or proxy 400, 410 or 422 as `RPC_ERROR`, ambiguous or not; a 401/403 as `PROVIDER_MISCONFIGURED`) into a retryable `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`; retryable provider errors pass unchanged, and Esplora's own 404s are already `null`. `observe`'s copy check runs under it; Task 9's proof source uses it for every proof.
- `isOwnCopy` authenticates the spender's bytes itself: under a quorum the answers are keyed on the txid they hash to, and bytes that do not hash to the spender's id are a retryable `PROVIDER_INCONSISTENT`; a missing transaction decides nothing (`PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`).
- `parseWalletOptions` only checks the options' shape. Whether the wallet derives its `changeAddress` (A19) needs the wallet's keys, so Task 8 checks it at build time; `fromPublicKey` never does, because the core also calls it with an xpub child key (`deriveAddress`).
